# Frontend to backend mapping

Status: DRAFT, scope 1.0 / architecture 1. These are planned contracts, not implemented screens. Canonical data: [frontend-catalog.json](frontend-catalog.json). Backend schemas and authorization are authoritative; navigation and UI guards never confer access.

540 explicit route/component/API mappings cover every user HTTP operation. Internal workers and signed provider webhook ingress have no browser screen. Repeated operations reflect legitimate role-specific consumers, not extra backend implementations.

## /

Surface: public. Main component: PublishedPage. Mounted composition: AsyncBoundary, PageHeader, PublishedPage.

| Component / operation | API / schemas | Application operation | Domain objects | Repository / integration ports | Persistence boundary | Requirements |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| PublishedPage / API-PUBLIC-PAGE | GET /api/v1/public/pages/{slug}; API_PUBLIC_PAGERequest → PublicPageView | PublicContentService.get_page | PublicPage, Program, Course, TeacherProfile, PublicationPolicy, ContactEnquiry, Cohort, Price | ContentRepository, CourseRepository, UserRepository, NotificationRepository, UnitOfWork, DeliveryRepository, PaymentRepository | Transaction infrastructure, accounts,credentials,teacher_profiles, cohorts,class_sessions,teacher_assignments, notifications,notification_deliveries, prices,payments,payment_events,refunds,purchase_documents,reconciliation_exceptions,privacy_exports(financial_export purpose); courses/cohorts read-only identity/title/status projection for finance price targets; curriculum/delivery mutation ownership remains in their modules, programs,courses,curriculum_revisions,course_modules,lessons,lesson_blocks,learning_resources, public_pages,public_page_revisions,policy_documents,contact_enquiries | LRN-001, WEB-001, WEB-002, WEB-003, WEB-004, WEB-007, WEB-008, WEB-009, WEB-012 |
